### [198] [Random Quantum Circuits Beyond Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02135)
*Shih-Han Hung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究随机量子电路中近似酉设计(approximate unitary designs)如何准确再现输出概率的完整分布,建立了矩匹配保证分布精度的定量界限,并证明局部不变性可带来指数级改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似酉设计通过匹配Haar随机酉矩阵的矩来模拟理想随机量子演化,但仅知道矩匹配精度不足以完全刻画单个输出概率分布的准确性。需要建立定量保证,明确矩匹配能在多大程度上再现完整的概率分布特性,以及哪些额外结构能显著提升这种再现精度。

Method: 1) 对n量子比特上的强ε-近似酉k-设计,利用Kolmogorov距离度量其输出概率分布与有限维Porter-Thomas分布(参数为1和2^n-1的beta分布)之间的偏差;2) 证明该偏差的上界为O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n)))+ε),并证明此界是最优的(精确到常数因子);3) 引入局部不变性(在log k+O(1)个量子比特上的酉平移下不变)作为额外结构;4) 证明局部不变性使对k的依赖从多项式改进为指数级,在Kolmogorov距离和全变差距离下分别达到2^(-Ω(k))+O(ε)和2^(-Ω(k))+O(ε log(2/ε))的误差。

Result: 1) 建立了近似酉k-设计输出概率分布的最优误差界:O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n)))+ε),证明了仅靠矩匹配无法获得实质性更好的一致界;2) 证明局部不变性带来指数级改进:误差从多项式衰减变为指数衰减2^(-Ω(k));3) 作为应用,证明了如果每个输出概率的边缘分布与Haar对应分布的Kolmogorov距离在η以内,则输出分布的期望Shannon熵与Haar值的偏差为O(√η)。

Conclusion: 本文精确刻画了近似酉设计通过矩匹配所能保证的分布准确性,证明了一般情况下的界是最优的,并识别出局部不变性这一关键额外结构可带来指数级改进。这些结果为理解随机量子电路的统计性质提供了定量基础,明确了从矩匹配到完整分布再现所需的结构条件,对量子计算和量子信息理论具有重要意义。

Abstract: Random quantum circuits aim to efficiently reproduce the statistical properties of ideal random quantum evolution. One approach is to construct approximate unitary designs, which match the moments of Haar-random unitaries up to a prescribed order with controlled error. In this work, we establish quantitative guarantees for how accurately these designs reproduce the full distributions of individual output probabilities. We show that, for every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design on $n$ qubits, the distribution of each individual output probability is within $O\left(\sqrt{\frac{2^n}{k(k+2^n)}}+\varepsilon\right)$ in Kolmogorov distance of the finite-dimensional Porter-Thomas distribution, a beta distribution with parameters $1$ and $2^n-1$. This bound is optimal up to constant factors, implying that a substantially better uniform bound requires additional structure. We further show that local invariance yields an exponential improvement in the dependence on $k$, with guarantees also in the stronger metric of total variation: every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design invariant under unitary translations acting on $\log k+O(1)$ qubits achieves error $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon)$ in Kolmogorov distance and $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon\log(2/\varepsilon))$ in total variation distance. These results identify both the distributional accuracy guaranteed by moment matching and the additional structure that substantially improves it. As an application, we show that if the marginal distribution of each output probability of a strong approximate design is within Kolmogorov distance $η$ of its Haar counterpart, then the expected Shannon entropy of the output distribution differs from its Haar value by $O(\sqrtη)$.

</details>


### [199] [One-Shot any Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02137)
*Andrew C. Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将CSS量子LDPC码转换为具有单次量子纠错能力的增强型CSS QLDPC码的构造方法,该方法在保持解码器独立阈值的同时,实现了错误抑制能力的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子纠错码在面对物理噪声和测量噪声时,需要多轮测量才能可靠纠错,这增加了实现复杂度。单次纠错(single-shot)技术可以仅用一轮测量完成纠错,但如何系统地构造具有良好阈值和错误抑制能力的单次纠错码仍是挑战。本文旨在开发一种通用的码转换方法,既能实现单次纠错,又能增强原有码的错误抑制性能。

Method: 文章采用码构造和理论分析相结合的方法。首先证明任何单次码都具有自纠正性质。然后提出一种系统的转换方案,将任意[[n,k]] CSS量子LDPC码转换为[[nm,k]] CSS QLDPC码。转换后的码配备了高效的局部解码器,其并行运行时间为O(log m)。通过理论分析,证明在联合局部随机物理噪声和测量噪声模型下,该码具有常数阈值p_RG和指数级错误抑制Ω(m^α)。进一步分析了当输入码本身具有单次解码器时的性能增强效果。

Result: 构造的[[nm,k]] CSS QLDPC码在阈值p_RG以下的噪声环境中,T轮纠错后的逻辑失败概率上界为O(Tn)exp[-Ω(m^α)]。当输入码配备阈值为p_c、错误抑制为Ω(n^β)的单次解码器时,输出码的错误抑制能力被增强至Ω(m^α n^β),同时保持解码器独立的阈值p_RG。若p_c < p_RG,则实现了阈值的提升。解码器具有O(log m)的并行时间复杂度,保证了实际应用的高效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了从普通CSS QLDPC码到单次纠错CSS QLDPC码的系统转换框架,证明了任何单次码的自纠正性质,并实现了错误抑制能力的乘法增强。该方法提供了常数噪声阈值、指数级错误抑制和高效的局部解码算法,为构建大规模容错量子计算系统提供了理论基础和实用工具。转换方法的通用性使其可应用于多种已知的量子LDPC码,推进了量子纠错码理论和实践的发展。

Abstract: We first provide a simple proof showing that any single-shot code is self-correcting. We then show that any $[[n,k]]$ CSS quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) code can be transformed into an $[[nm,k]]$ CSS QLDPC code with single-shot (SS) quantum error correction and an efficient local decoder which runs in $O(\log m)$ parallel time. Below a constant threshold $p_{\rm RG}>0$ for joint local stochastic physical and measurement noise, the logical failure probability over $T$ correction rounds is bounded by \begin{equation*}
  O(T n)\exp[-Ω(m^α)] \end{equation*} for a constant $α>0$; we say that the \textit{threshold} is $p_{\rm RG}$ and \textit{error suppression} is $Ω(m^α)$. Moreover, if the input code is equipped with a single-shot decoder with threshold $p_c>0$ and error suppression $Ω(n^β)$, then the output code \textit{enhances} the error suppression to $Ω(m^α n^β)$, while maintaining the decoder-independent threshold $p_{\rm RG}$, and thus \textit{enhanced} if $p_c < p_{\rm RG}$.

</details>


### [200] [A provable quantum advantage for approximate optimization via decoded quantum interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02145)
*Maximilian J. Kramer,Elies Gil-Fuster,Benjamin D. M. Jones,Jens Eisert,Franz J. Schreiber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在预言机设置下证明了解码量子干涉测量(DQI)相比所有多项式时间经典算法在近似优化问题上具有可证明的量子优势,通过折叠最优多项式交集(folded OPI)问题展示了严格的近似比差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码量子干涉测量(DQI)是在量子计算机上解决近似优化问题的新范式,具有强大的性能保证并利用了优化与编码理论之间的对偶性。然而,一个核心问题是DQI是否能够可证明地优于所有多项式时间经典算法。本文旨在建立这种量子优势的理论证明。

Method: 本文在预言机设置下考虑折叠最优多项式交集(folded OPI)优化任务,其中接受集合随机选择并通过成员预言机访问。研究构建于Jordan等人的DQI近似优化框架之上,将Yamakawa和Zhandry用于精确搜索预言机分离的经典下界方法扩展到近似场景。此外,基于Sun和Wootters、Horinaga和Yamakawa以及Jo的最新研究进展,进一步展示了DQI算法的改进版本在folded OPI问题上实现更大的性能差距。

Result: 研究在任何多项式时间经典算法可达到的近似比与DQI算法达到的近似比之间建立了严格差距。具体而言,对于码率0.3的情况,DQI算法达到约0.85的期望得分,改进版DQI算法达到约0.95的期望得分,而经典算法的阈值仅为0.65。在采样实例上以恒定概率超过经典阈值任何固定量需要超多项式数量的经典成员查询。改进版DQI算法实现了更强的量子分离。

Conclusion: 本文成功证明了解码量子干涉测量在预言机设置下相对于多项式时间经典算法的可证明量子优势。通过折叠最优多项式交集问题,展示了量子算法在近似优化任务中可以实现经典算法无法达到的近似比,为量子计算在优化问题上的优越性提供了理论支撑。改进版DQI算法进一步扩大了这一优势差距,强化了量子分离结果。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a novel paradigm for tackling approximate optimization problems on quantum computers. This framework comes with strong performance guarantees and exploits a well-established duality between optimization and coding theory. A central question, however, is whether DQI can actually provably outperform all polynomial-time classical algorithms. In this work, we establish such an advantage in an oracle setting: we consider an optimization task called folded optimal polynomial intersection (folded OPI), where the acceptance sets are chosen randomly and accessed through membership oracles. We establish a strict gap between the approximation ratio achievable by any polynomial-time classical algorithm and the approximation ratio achieved by the DQI algorithm. Our proof builds on Jordan et al.'s DQI framework for approximate optimization and extends the classical lower-bound method underlying Yamakawa and Zhandry's exact-search oracle separation to approximation. Building on recent developments by Sun and Wootters, Horinaga and Yamakawa, and Jo, we further show that a modified version of the DQI algorithm achieves a strictly larger gap on the folded OPI problem, yielding an even stronger quantum separation. As a concrete example, for code rate $0.3$, DQI and the modified algorithm achieve expected scores of approximately $0.85$ and $0.95$, respectively. In contrast, exceeding the classical threshold of $0.65$ by any fixed amount with constant probability on sampled instances requires super-polynomially many classical membership queries.

</details>


### [201] [Polynomial-time additive-error estimation of output probabilities for shallow quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02146)
*Matthew Coudron,Michael J. Gullans,Jon Nelson,Joel Rajakumar,Shi Jie Samuel Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性经典算法,能够在多项式时间poly(n, 1/ε)内估计常数深度量子电路的输出振幅平方,显著改进了现有算法的时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的估计常数深度量子电路输出振幅的经典算法存在时间复杂度过高的问题:通用电路需要n^O(log(n))时间,几何局部电路需要n^O(log(log(n)))时间,二维几何局部电路需要n^O(1)时间。需要一种更高效的确定性经典算法来处理具有任意连接性的常数深度量子电路。

Method: 提出了一种确定性经典算法,用于估计常数深度量子电路U作用在初始态|0^n⟩上,得到任意n比特输出串x的振幅平方|⟨x|U|0^n⟩|^2。该算法适用于由有界扇入门构成且具有任意连接性的常数深度量子电路,能够在多项式时间复杂度poly(n, 1/ε)内达到加性误差ε的估计精度。

Result: 算法实现了多项式时间复杂度poly(n, 1/ε),相比于现有技术取得了显著改进:将通用常数深度电路的时间复杂度从n^O(log(n))降低到多项式级别;对于几何局部电路,从n^O(log(log(n)))改进到多项式级别;对于二维几何局部电路,从n^O(1)改进到严格的多项式时间。该算法适用于任意连接性的量子电路,具有更广泛的适用范围。

Conclusion: 本文提出的确定性经典算法在估计常数深度量子电路输出振幅方面取得了突破性进展,将时间复杂度降低到多项式级别poly(n, 1/ε),显著优于所有先前的最优算法。这一结果表明,对于常数深度量子电路,经典算法在某些计算任务上可以达到高效的性能,为理解量子计算与经典计算的边界提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: We give a deterministic classical algorithm that estimates $|\langle x|U|0^n\rangle|^2$ to additive error $\varepsilon$ in $\mathrm{poly}(n, 1/\varepsilon)$ time, where $U$ is a constant-depth quantum circuit comprised of gates with bounded fan-in and arbitrary connectivity, and $x$ is an arbitrary $n$-bit output string. This improves over prior state-of-the-art algorithms that takes $n^{O(log(n))}$ time for the same task, $n^{O(log(log(n))}$ when $U$ is geometrically local, and $n^{O(1)}$ for 2D geometrically-local circuits.

</details>


### [202] [The Robustness of QAC0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02154)
*Daniel Grier,Jackson Morris,Kewen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子常数深度电路类 QAC⁰ 在错误容忍度和门集合修改方面的鲁棒性。研究表明:1) 通过精确振幅放大技术,QAC⁰ 可以无误差地模拟 TC⁰;2) QAC⁰ 的计算能力对单量子比特门的限制具有鲁棒性,仅使用广义 Toffoli 门、S 门和 Hadamard 门即可近似实现任意 QAC⁰ 电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子常数深度电路类(QAC⁰)在计算布尔函数时通常允许非零误差,但这种误差是否真正必要尚不清楚。此外,QAC⁰ 的计算能力在多大程度上依赖于可以在电路任意位置使用任意单量子比特门也是一个关键问题。研究这些问题有助于深入理解量子常数深度电路的本质特性和计算边界。

Method: 1) 错误消除方法:将精确振幅放大技术创新性地应用于多副本场景,以消除并行 W-测试中固有的误差,从而构造出精确的 QAC⁰ 电路(EQAC⁰);2) 门集合鲁棒性分析:通过电路构造和理论分析,证明任意 QAC⁰ 电路可以仅用广义 Toffoli 门、S 门和 Hadamard 门近似实现,并从原始电路的经典描述高效构造近似电路。

Result: 1) 证明了 EQAC⁰(精确 QAC⁰)能够在多项式多个经典输入副本的情况下精确模拟 TC⁰;2) 对于每个固定素数 p,精确 QAC⁰ 可以计算 AC⁰[p] 之外的全布尔函数;3) 证明了 QAC⁰ 对单量子比特门集合的限制具有鲁棒性,任意 QAC⁰ 电路可以用受限门集合(广义 Toffoli 门、S 门、Hadamard 门)高效地近似实现。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了 QAC⁰ 的两个重要鲁棒性特征:首先,通过精确振幅放大技术,可以完全消除 QAC⁰ 电路计算布尔函数时的误差,构造出精确的量子常数深度电路,从而能够精确模拟 TC⁰ 并计算超出 AC⁰[p] 的函数;其次,QAC⁰ 的计算能力对单量子比特门的选择并不敏感,即使将门集合限制为广义 Toffoli 门、S 门和 Hadamard 门,仍能高效地近似实现任意 QAC⁰ 电路。这些结果深化了对量子常数深度计算本质的理解。

Abstract: In this work we study the robustness of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ with respect to error tolerance and modifications to its gate-set. First, we investigate whether the non-zero error typically allowed for $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuits computing Boolean functions is truly necessary. We show that the error inherent in the parallel $W$-test of \cite{grier_morris_wu} can be eliminated entirely via a novel application of exact amplitude amplification in the many-copies context. Consequently, we find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ can \textit{exactly} simulate $\mathsf{TC}^0$ with polynomially many copies of the classical input and that for every fixed prime $p$ exact $\mathsf{QAC}^0$, $\mathsf{EQAC}^0$, can compute total Boolean functions outside of $\mathsf{AC}^0[p]$.
  Second, we ask to what extent the computational power of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ follows from the fact that arbitrary single-qubit gates may be used at any point in the circuit. We find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ is in fact robust to restrictions on which single-qubit gates are permitted: every $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit can be approximately implemented by a $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit consisting of just generalized Toffoli, $S$, and Hadamard gates. Moreover, this approximating circuit can be constructed efficiently from a classical description of the original circuit.

</details>


### [203] [Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians Using a Local Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02157)
*Suying Liu,Zitai Xu,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Xiaodi Wu,Yu-Xin Wang,Zhi-Yuan Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅需单个可测量量子比特即可学习N量子比特有界度两体哈密顿量的所有O(N)个独立参数的高效协议,通过量子信号处理合成的鲁棒SWAP门实现相干态转移,在N量子比特链上达到总查询时间Õ(N)和海森堡极限精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿学习协议通常假设可以直接测量整个系统,这在实际量子系统中难以实现。本文旨在解决如何仅通过局部测量接口(单个可测量量子比特)来学习大规模多体哈密顿量参数的挑战,同时保持最优的精度缩放和时间效率。

Method: 利用快速单量子比特控制和连接的参考骨干网络,通过量子信号处理(QSP)合成鲁棒的SWAP门,将在远程哈密顿参数下演化的量子态相干转移到可测量量子比特。关键是该转移过程无需预先校准中间链路的哈密顿参数。采用并行学习架构,对N量子比特链实现总查询时间Õ(N),同时保持海森堡极限精度缩放。该框架可进一步扩展到任意有界度相互作用图。

Result: 在N量子比特链上,该协议在精度和信息传播方面达到基本下界(仅差对数因子),总查询时间为Õ(N),能够以海森堡极限精度学习所有O(N)个独立的两体哈密顿参数。方法可扩展到任意有界度交互图结构,实现了广泛多体哈密顿参数的可扩展学习。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一条可扩展的路径,通过仅使用局部测量接口即可学习大量多体哈密顿参数。该方法突破了现有哈密顿学习需要全局测量访问的限制,通过量子信号处理实现的鲁棒SWAP门和并行学习架构,在保持最优精度缩放的同时实现了近线性的时间复杂度,为实际量子系统中的哈密顿层析提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Hamiltonian learning provides a systematic framework for reconstructing unknown quantum dynamics. However, existing protocols typically assume direct measurement access to the entire system. With fast single-qubit control and a connected reference backbone, we show that a single measurable qubit suffices to learn all $O(N)$ independent parameters of a bounded-degree two-body Hamiltonian on $N$ qubits at the Heisenberg limit. Crucially, our protocol uses robust SWAP gates synthesized by quantum signal processing, enabling coherent transfer of states evolving under distant Hamiltonian parameters to the measurable qubit. This transfer requires no prior calibration of the Hamiltonian parameters of the intermediate links. A parallel learning architecture achieves total query time $\widetilde{O}(N)$ for an $N$-qubit chain, while retaining Heisenberg-limited precision scaling. On the chain, these scalings match the fundamental precision and information-propagation lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. The framework further extends to arbitrary bounded-degree interaction graphs. Our results establish a scalable route to learning an extensive number of many-body Hamiltonian parameters through only a local measurement interface.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond Light Cones: State Preparation Complexity in Quantum Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02166)
*Omar Al-Ghattas,David Gamarnik,Bobak T Kiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种研究密集量子p-spin哈密顿量中态制备复杂度的新方法,通过引入有效轮廓复杂度(effective profile complexity)概念,超越了仅基于电路光锥的界限。研究证明了在固定p值下,具有均匀有界二次有效轮廓复杂度的量子态类与基态能量保持Ω(√n)的能量差距,并建立了多种电路复杂度下界,包括门数量、深度-宽度权衡以及魔术态层级的障碍结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子态制备复杂度研究主要依赖于电路光锥(circuit lightcone)方法,但这种方法在处理某些电路结构时存在局限性,特别是当Clifford电路可以将局部可观测量扩散到整个系统时。为了突破这一限制,需要发展超越光锥界限的新分析工具,以更精确地刻画在p-spin哈密顿量中达到接近基态能量所需的电路资源。此外,需要理解不同量子态类(如magic层级中的不同层次)在能量优化中的本质限制。

Method: 核心方法是引入"有效轮廓复杂度"(effective profile complexity)概念,该复杂度通过Pauli轮廓的度量熵(metric entropy)来定义。Pauli轮廓记录了所有恰好支撑在p个量子比特上的Pauli算符的期望值。证明技术结合了两个关键工具:(1)改编自Berta等人的非对称量子de Finetti定理;(2)高斯过程熵界(Gaussian process entropy bounds)。通过分析不同量子态类的有效轮廓复杂度阶数(二次、次二次等),建立了这些态类与基态能量之间的能量间隙下界,并将其转化为电路复杂度的下界。

Result: 主要结果包括:(1)达到接近基态能量需要Ω(n²/log n)个单比特和双比特门,即使允许任意可丢弃的辅助量子比特;(2)建立了深度-宽度权衡关系;(3)得到了纠缠深度和矩阵乘积态(MPS)键维度的下界;(4)对Parham魔术层级的每个固定层次的两种方向都证明了障碍结果(总电路宽度为O(n));(5)对于第一层反向魔术态(浅电路后接无限制Clifford电路),即使允许任意数量的干净辅助比特,在固定浅电路深度下仍有下界;(6)证明了具有o(n)个T门的Clifford+T电路相比乘积稳定子态在主导阶能量上没有优势,即使有无限制的Clifford操作和任意可丢弃辅助比特。

Conclusion: 本文通过有效轮廓复杂度框架,为量子态制备复杂度研究提供了一个超越光锥方法的强大新工具。研究结果表明,在密集p-spin哈密顿量中达到基态能量存在本质的电路资源障碍,这些障碍在多个维度上体现(门数量、深度、宽度、纠缠结构、魔术资源等)。特别地,即使允许Clifford电路这样可以全局传播信息的操作,以及辅助量子比特资源,这些下界依然成立,揭示了量子计算中态制备的基本复杂度特征。该方法为理解量子优势的资源需求和限制提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We introduce a method for studying state preparation complexity in dense quantum $p$-spin Hamiltonians on $n$ qubits, going beyond bounds based only on circuit lightcones. The key input is the class's effective profile complexity, which is derived from the metric entropy of its Pauli profiles. These profiles record expectations of all Pauli operators supported on exactly $p$ qubits. Classes with uniformly bounded quadratic effective profile complexity remain separated from the ground-state energy by a positive multiple of $\sqrt n$ for sufficiently large fixed $p$. At subquadratic effective profile complexity, the class cannot outperform a suitable benchmark class at leading order, with product states providing a universal benchmark. The proof combines an adaptation of a nonsymmetric quantum de Finetti theorem of Berta et al. (arXiv:1810.12197) with Gaussian process entropy bounds.
  Applying this framework, we show that attaining near-ground-state energy requires $Ω(n^2/\log n)$ one- and two-qubit gates, even with arbitrary discardable ancillas. We also obtain depth-width tradeoffs, entanglement-depth and matrix product state bond-dimension lower bounds, and obstructions for both orientations at every fixed level of Parham's magic hierarchy (arXiv:2504.19966), with total circuit width $O(n)$. In first-level reverse magic, a shallow circuit is followed by an unrestricted Clifford circuit. The latter can spread local observables across the system, preventing a direct application of small-lightcone bounds. For this first-level class, our bounds also allow arbitrarily many clean ancillas at fixed shallow-circuit depth. A sharper benchmark shows that Clifford+$T$ circuits with $o(n)$ $T$-gates have no leading-order energy advantage over product stabilizer states, even with unrestricted Clifford operations and arbitrary discardable ancillas.

</details>


### [205] [Polynomial-time classical and quantum simulation of quantum impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02167)
*Jiaqing Jiang,Nathan Ju,Ojas Parekh,Chaithanya Rayudu,Andrew Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了量子杂质模型模拟的计算复杂度，证明了静态性质可被经典计算机高效计算（多项式时间），而动态性质则需要量子计算机，从而界定了该类问题的经典与量子可解边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型是相互作用量子物质的范式模型和现代电子结构方法的关键计算基元，但其模拟的计算复杂度边界尚不清晰。需要明确哪些问题可被经典计算机高效求解，哪些问题需要量子优势，以指导量子计算在该领域的应用方向。

Method: 采用计算复杂度理论分析方法：(1) 对静态性质，构造经典算法计算基态能量（加性精度δ，时间复杂度poly(n,δ^-1)）和配分函数（逆温度β下相对精度δ，时间复杂度poly(n,β,δ^-1)）；(2) 对动态性质，以非平衡格林函数计算为例，通过复杂度归约证明其捕获了量子计算的全部能力（即使在有限温度下）。

Result: 静态性质模拟：基态能量估计从拟多项式时间改进至多项式时间，首次建立热平衡态模拟的严格多项式时间保证。动态性质模拟：证明非平衡格林函数计算在经典计算机上困难但在量子计算机上容易，该问题具有量子计算完全性。这排除了静态性质计算中超多项式量子加速的可能性。

Conclusion: 量子杂质模型的静态性质（基态能量、热力学量）可被经典计算机在多项式时间内高效求解，不存在量子优势；但动态性质（如非平衡格林函数）的模拟需要量子计算机，为非平衡杂质物理模拟中的量子优势提供了明确途径，界定了该问题类别的经典-量子可解性边界。

Abstract: Quantum impurity models are paradigmatic models of interacting quantum matter, as well as key computational primitives for modern electronic-structure methods. They describe a small subsystem of interacting fermions coupled to a large, noninteracting bath. We perform a comprehensive study of the computational complexity of simulating impurity models, delineating the boundary between classical and quantum tractability for this class of problems. Our main finding is that static properties of quantum impurity models can be calculated efficiently on a classical computer. Specifically, we give classical algorithms that (1) estimate the ground-state energy to additive precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,δ^{-1})$, and (2) estimate the partition function at inverse temperature $β$ to relative precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,β,δ^{-1})$, where $n$ is the system size. These results improve the previous best-known complexity for ground-state energy estimation from quasipolynomial to polynomial time, while establishing for the first time rigorous polynomial-time guarantees for simulating impurity models in thermal equilibrium. On the other hand, we find that simulating dynamical properties of impurity models is hard for classical computers but easy on a quantum computer. As a canonical example, we show that computing their nonequilibrium Green's functions captures the full power of quantum computation, even at finite temperature. Taken together, our results rule out superpolynomial quantum speedups for computing static properties, but provide an avenue for quantum advantage in simulating impurity physics out of equilibrium.

</details>


### [206] [Robust exponential lower bounds for fermionic and bosonic Gaussian ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02172)
*Fuchuan Wei,Kong-Wing Wu,Zhengwei Liu,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了玻色子和费米子系统中的高斯秩问题,证明了非高斯态的近似边界高斯秩具有鲁棒的指数下界,这为基于分解的经典模拟量子系统设定了基本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算优势的核心在于理解经典模拟的能力与局限性。相干分解为可经典处理的自由态是主流模拟范式,但证明分解秩的强下界是一个极其困难的数学问题。本文旨在研究玻色子和费米子系统中的高斯版本问题,建立高斯秩的指数下界,以揭示非高斯性对经典模拟复杂度的根本影响。

Method: 研究采用了多种技术方法:对于费米子系统,将问题约化到四模态并结合Majorana谱界;对于玻色子系统,使用高斯后选择结合基于熵的秩界方法。通过这些技术,对有限模态上的每个纯非高斯态(费米子情况下具有确定宇称)进行分析,研究其张量幂的近似边界高斯秩增长规律。并以四模态费米子GHZ态和玻色子单光子态为具体实例进行验证。

Result: 证明了对于任意有限模态上的纯非高斯态,在固定范数误差小于1的条件下,其张量幂的近似边界高斯秩至少呈指数增长。对四模态费米子GHZ态和玻色子单光子态推导出明确的指数下界。玻色子结果不需要对目标态的能量做任何假设,具有普适性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明,非高斯性普遍导致指数级的高斯分解复杂度,这为基于分解的玻色子和费米子系统经典模拟设定了基本的理论限制。这些发现深化了对量子计算优势本质的理解,证明了非高斯资源在量子计算中的不可替代性和经典模拟的固有困难性。

Abstract: The power and limitations of classical simulation are central to understanding quantum computational advantages. A leading simulation paradigm is based on coherent decomposition into classically tractable free states where the decomposition rank determines the simulation complexity. Proving strong lower bounds on this number is a notoriously difficult and mathematically rich problem, as exemplified by the qubit stabilizer rank problem. Here, we study the Gaussian version of this problem in both bosonic and fermionic systems and establish robust exponential lower bounds on Gaussian rank. In particular, we prove that for every pure non-Gaussian state on finitely many modes, with definite parity in the fermionic case, the approximate border Gaussian rank of its tensor powers grows at least exponentially at any fixed norm error below one. Our proofs combine reduction to four modes with Majorana spectral bounds for fermions, and Gaussian postselection with an entropy-based rank bound for bosons. As concrete examples, we derive explicit exponential lower bounds for the four-mode fermionic GHZ state and the bosonic single-photon state. The bosonic results require no assumption on the energy of the target state. Our results show that non-Gaussianity universally entails exponential Gaussian decomposition complexity, setting fundamental limitations on decomposition-based classical simulation of bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [207] [From Permutation Symmetry to Communication Bounds and Additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02176)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Debbie Leung,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了完全置换不变性如何限制量子信道中跨信道使用的相关性优势。证明了对于有限维无记忆信道,置换不变输入下优化的相干信息在有限块长度达到最大值,建立了强逆定理,并将可加性结果推广到输出熵控制领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信中跨信道使用的相关性可以提高通信速率,使得任意大块的优化成为确定量子容量的核心难题。需要理解置换不变性这一对称性如何限制这种优势,以及如何简化量子容量的渐近优化问题。

Method: 采用De Finetti约化和置换协方差技术,在统一的变分框架下分析置换不变输入。通过研究完全有界范数和夹心Rényi信道量之间的共同结构,系统性地证明可乘性和可加性结果。对纯源纠缠生成建立强逆定理,并分析输出熵的渐近行为。

Result: 证明了在完全置换不变性下,每次使用的优化相干信息收敛到单次使用最大值,并在有限块长度达到上确界。建立了强逆定理:当固定速率超过单次使用相干信息最大值时,保真度趋于零。证明了置换不变输入下,相关性无法使每次使用的输出熵渐近地低于单次使用最小值,该结果对Rényi熵(阶数大于1)和von Neumann熵均成立。

Conclusion: 完全置换不变性显著限制了量子信道中块编码的优势:虽然有限块的超可加性仍然可能,但渐近优化简化为单信道使用。这将对称性的作用从通信界扩展到输出熵控制,为量子容量优化提供了重要的理论简化,并在统一框架下系统地解释了已知的可乘性和可加性结果。

Abstract: Correlations across channel uses can improve quantum communication rates, making optimization over arbitrarily large blocks a central difficulty in determining quantum capacity. We show how full permutation invariance limits this advantage for every finite-dimensional memoryless channel. The optimized coherent information per use converges to the single-use maximum and consequently attains its supremum at a finite block length. Thus finite-block superadditivity remains possible, while the asymptotic optimization reduces to a single channel use. We also establish a strong converse for pure-source entanglement generation with permutation-invariant inputs: at any fixed rate above the single-use coherent-information maximum, the fidelity tends to zero. Beyond these communication bounds, we identify a common structure underlying completely bounded norms and the sandwiched Rényi channel quantities governing discrimination against a replacer and entanglement-assisted communication. De Finetti reduction and permutation covariance yield systematic alternative proofs of their known multiplicativity and additivity results within a shared variational framework. We further show that, for permutation-invariant inputs, correlations cannot sustain an asymptotic reduction in output entropy per use below the single-use minimum. This entropy limit holds for Rényi orders greater than one and for the von Neumann entropy, extending the role of symmetry from communication bounds to the control of output entropy.

</details>


### [208] [Learning SYK Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02178)
*Anurag Anshu,Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sitan Chen,Yeongwoo Hwang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究从Gibbs态副本中学习密集Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK)哈密顿量的问题,证明了利用随机平均场结构可以克服传统哈密顿量学习算法的局限性,在常数温度下用多项式样本数实现逆多项式精度学习,并在低温情况下构造了拟多项式时间学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的哈密顿量学习算法通常依赖于几何局域性或有界相互作用度,但这些方法不适用于SYK模型。在SYK模型中,每个四次相互作用与Θ(n³)个其他相互作用重叠,呈现出高度非局域性和密集相互作用的特征。因此,需要开发新的方法来克服这一障碍,利用SYK模型特有的随机平均场结构来实现有效的哈密顿量学习。

Method: 研究采用了两种不同的算法策略:1)样本高效算法:利用SYK模型的随机平均场结构,在任意常数温度下,通过多项式数量的Gibbs态副本样本来学习整个哈密顿量;2)拟多项式时间算法:针对逆温度为足够小常数的情况,构造了一个在性质上与样本高效算法截然不同的拟多项式时间学习算法。这些方法充分利用了SYK耦合的随机性和统计特性。

Result: 在任意常数温度下,以高概率(相对于SYK耦合)证明了可以使用多项式数量的样本将整个哈密顿量学习到逆多项式精度。对于低温情况(逆温度为足够小的常数),成功构造了拟多项式时间的学习算法。这些结果表明,尽管SYK模型具有密集的相互作用结构,但其随机平均场特性使得高效学习成为可能。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了密集SYK哈密顿量的可学习性,突破了传统哈密顿量学习算法对几何局域性和有界相互作用度的依赖。通过利用SYK模型的随机平均场结构,实现了样本复杂度和时间复杂度上的有效学习算法。这一工作为学习非局域、高度相互作用的量子多体系统开辟了新途径,对量子机器学习和量子多体物理的交叉研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning the dense Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian from copies of its Gibbs state. Existing algorithms for Hamiltonian learning typically rely on geometric locality or bounded interaction degree and therefore do not apply to SYK, where each quartic interaction overlaps with $Θ(n^3)$ others. We show that this obstruction can be overcome by exploiting the random mean-field structure of the model. At any constant temperature, we prove that with high probability over the SYK couplings, the entire Hamiltonian can be learned to inverse-polynomial accuracy using polynomially many samples. Furthermore, when the inverse temperature is restricted to be a sufficiently small constant, we construct a quasipolynomial-time learning algorithm which is qualitatively different from the sample-efficient algorithm.

</details>


### [209] [Single-Particle Spectral Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02183)
*Adrian Chapman,Charles Derby,Steven T. Flammia,Yeongwoo Hwang,Joel Klassen,Calum McCartney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在全局幺正变换混淆下的哈密顿量学习问题，提出了SPICES算法，通过Hadamard测试和经典后处理高效恢复被隐藏的非相互作用模式的单粒子能谱，为材料模拟中的量子应用提供了实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料建模中，许多经典方法（如平均场理论、格林函数、密度泛函理论）涉及非相互作用哈密顿量的学习。然而，实际物理系统中这种非相互作用结构常被未知的全局幺正变换所隐藏。现有方法需要重构整个幺正变换U才能获取能量权重E_i，这在计算上非常困难。因此需要一种能够直接恢复能谱分布而无需重构U的高效方法，以推动量子计算在材料模拟中的实际应用。

Method: 提出SPICES（通过轮廓估计器进行单粒子推断）算法，该方法结合了Hadamard测试和新颖的经典后处理程序。核心思想是利用单粒子能量与配分函数在复逆温度下零点的傅里叶频率之间的对应关系。具体而言，通过Hadamard测试获取量子态信息，然后使用经典后处理分析配分函数的解析结构，从傅里叶频率中提取单粒子能谱，从而在不重构全局幺正变换U的情况下直接恢复能量权重E_i的谱分布。

Result: 证明了该哈密顿量学习问题是DQC1-困难的，表明即使具有非相互作用结构，问题本身仍具有计算复杂性。在特定的自然假设条件下，SPICES算法能够在Wasserstein距离度量下高效恢复谱分布。该方法简单实用，适合在量子设备上执行，并且准粒子能谱在下游材料计算中普遍存在，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的SPICES方法为在全局幺正混淆下学习非相互作用哈密顿量的能谱提供了一条高效且实用的途径。尽管该问题具有DQC1-困难的计算复杂性，SPICES通过巧妙利用配分函数的解析性质和傅里叶分析，绕过了完全重构幺正变换的需求。鉴于准粒子能谱在材料模拟计算中的核心地位，以及该方法的简洁性和可实施性，SPICES有望成为量子计算在材料科学领域应用的重要工具。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian learning in a novel setting, where we are promised a physical structure that is obfuscated by a global unitary. In particular, we consider the task of learning the weights $E_i$ of a Hamiltonian $H=U^{\dagger}(\sum_{i \in I} E_i n_i + H_O)U$ comprised of a set of non-interacting modes $I$ and an arbitrary ``spectator'' $H_O$ acting on a disjoint set of modes $O$. The non-interacting structure is hidden by an unknown global unitary $U$, and our goal is to recover the $E_i$ without reconstructing $U$. Such Hamiltonians are well-motivated in the setting of materials modeling, where they find application for classical methods such as mean-field theory, Green's functions, and density functional theory. We show that this problem is DQC1-hard, despite the apparent simplification given by the non-interacting structure. We then introduce SPICES (single-particle inference via contour estimators), which combines a Hadamard test with a novel classical postprocessing procedure to efficiently recover the spectral distribution in Wasserstein distance under certain natural assumptions. The method exploits the correspondence between single-particle energies of the individual quasiparticles and the Fourier frequencies governing zeros of the partition function at complex inverse temperature. As quasiparticle spectra feature ubiquitously in downstream materials calculations, and with SPICES being simple and practical to execute, we expect that SPICES is a promising route to quantum applications in materials simulation.

</details>
